## PRIOR (2026-07-22 — SESSION 310: **THE CONFIRM FLOOR** — Bouts W/C/D on a full autonomous mandate; a verified one-click manuscript-destruction path closed, the writing-tree delete gesture shipped, the first room-nav tests, story-tier search, and the knowledge link-all lift; gates: typecheck 0 + **1485 vitest** + full e2e + builds clean; full record `Notes/2026-07-22-s310-bout-w-design.md`)

- **A ONE-CLICK MANUSCRIPT DESTRUCTION PATH, FOUND AND CLOSED.** Deleting a Book from the writing room cascade-deletes every chapter and scene beneath it (`findDependents` walks `parent_narrative`; cascade defaults true) — and the ONLY guard was `confirm_before_delete`, a user preference that can be switched OFF in Settings → Elements. **Verified live before fixing** (real folder store, real clicks, `window.confirm` spy): one click, **0 confirms**, **8 of 9 elements gone from disk**, no undo anywhere in atlas. The fix is a **confirm FLOOR**: any delete that cascades always confirms. That is not an override of the user's choice — the setting reads "Ask for confirmation before deleting **an element**", singular, so destroying a subtree was never inside its opt-out. It also just aligns the header with the rest of the app: `map-index-tab.ts:555` (unconditional, names its pin cascade), `share-room.ts:166` ("this is a public, destructive action"), and `writing-sidebar.ts:603` ("no Ctrl+Z") all already had floors. The header was the lone outlier, and it is the one wired to the writing room, where the cascade is an entire manuscript. Conventions **#49c/#49d**.
- **THE SENTENCE NAMES WHAT DIES, IN THE USER'S OWN WORDS.** Was: "This will also remove 15 dependent elements." Now: *Delete "The Hegemony Cycle"? This also deletes 2 chapters, 4 scenes and 1 other narrative. This cannot be undone.* — tier counts from the world's configured labels (renamed labels included), pluralised correctly at 1. **The load-bearing subtlety**: the count walks ALL narratives like the cascade does, NOT the writing tree. The tree filters by tier label, so a free-texted narrative is invisible there and still deleted — those are named as "N other narratives". Confirmed live: the test world's off-tree `Interlude` was destroyed by the old path and is named by the new one. `narrative-delete.test.ts` asserts the kernel's walk equals `MockFolderStore.findDependents`, an invariant that opens both files.
- **BOUT W — the writing-tree Delete gesture** (the banked ask) shipped, routed through the same one rule site so the two gestures cannot drift. **The banked spec was wrong on two of three claims and re-derived**: writing-room delete ALREADY existed (the header trash), and "children stay, become top-level" would have contradicted a correct, reasoned cascade (orphans carry a dangling FK and re-surface as loose roots; they would push `invalid_link` to keel). Cascade kept; the design question was never what to delete, it was what the user is told first. **Navigation ordering is load-bearing**: deleting from the tree can destroy the narrative open in a mounted TipTap editor, so the handler navigates away BEFORE the delete. Verified under a real pending edit — typed unsaved words into a scene, deleted its parent chapter, and the scene did not resurrect (`persistSnapshot` bails on the URL lookup, `writing-workspace.ts:334`). Both halves of the fix revert-checked separately, 4 loud failures each.
- **BOUT C — guards** (agent-built, keeper-reviewed 100% against code, one mutation-tested): first tests for `room-nav.ts` (268 lines, zero prior coverage, two live bugs shipped from it in a week), `elementMatchesQuery` units, and a `TOUR_STEPS` guard. **The tour guard was strengthened by hand**: the agent's version compared TOUR_STEPS against a map transcribed into the test file, which cannot see a change made on the other side of the coupling — it now also reads `tour.spec.ts` off disk and asserts every `nextStep(page, N)` is in range and that the walk reaches the final step, with a guard-the-guard test so a changed call shape cannot leave it asserting nothing.
- **BOUT D — story-tier search** (`element-search.ts`): prose is now findable from both the palette and the sidebar, ranked LAST (30, below description) so a word buried in a chapter never outranks a name. **Perf measured, not assumed**: a paired bench on a worst-case 588-element world holding 2 MB of prose moved a full-world query 0.090 ms → 0.705 ms. 7.8x relative, 4% of a 16 ms frame budget. Verified live: searching "saurians" (a word only in prose) finds the scene with a readable snippet.
- **BOUT D — the knowledge link-all corner**, lifted to `middle/prose-linkall-corner.ts` and mounted on BOTH surfaces. The knowledge room mounts the same editor with the same autolink plugin into a host already carrying the writing prose class, and had simply never been given the button (the S308 "decision applied to ONE surface" class, logged and now closed). Own-eyes verified: pill with live count 2, click links both mentions, and **the visible prose is byte-identical afterwards** — THE LABEL LAW holds on the new surface for free, because both routes share `promotionLabel`.
- **THE GATE LANDED LATE AND WAS RIGHT TWICE** (3 opus lenses, returned after the bouts had shipped; both findings fixed and revert-checked). **(1) HIGH — the cascade bypassed each dependent's write chain.** `deleteElement` serializes on the TARGET's id (`folder-store-fs.ts:902`) and then unlinked descendants directly from inside that slot; `PerIdWriteChain` is per-id by design, so a concurrent `updateElement(childId)` was unserialized against that child's deletion and its write could RECREATE the file (restoring `fileIndex`/`bodyCache`) after the tombstone was logged — next sync round pushes a DELETE for an element that still exists locally. **I had noticed this bypass myself while reading the cascade and moved on**; the gate's value was refusing to let a dropped thread stay dropped. Fixed in BOTH stores, deadlock-guarded on `dep.id === id`. **The test story matters**: the behavioural resurrection test PASSED against the unfixed cascade (a false green — the fake's update has one yield where the cascade has several), and still could not fail after I added the tombstone-write yield the fake had been missing. Honest close per S258: behavioural tests lock the observable contract, a **source scan is the actual lock** (revert-checked, 2 failures when the chain is removed). **(2) MED — the new tree gesture could SOFT-LOCK the tour**: the writing beat gates advance on chips inside a revealed Chapter, the tree is reachable while touring, and deleting its parent Book made the gate permanently unsatisfiable with no self-heal. Delete is now suppressed while `isTourFlagActive()`, asserted as a present/absent PAIR and revert-checked. Accepted unchanged (pre-existing, non-blocking): the confirm counts from memory while the delete counts from disk · 60 tombstones is 60 sequential read-modify-writes with no bulk delete path · `writing-workspace-recents.ts` never prunes deleted ids.
- ⚡ **NEXT**: Captain's copy pass · og:image pick · the World-green trade (Skeld recommends keeping `#008300`) · a `show` redeploy is still owed. Bout W/C/D are closed; the remaining stepback runway items (icon() migration, dead CSS, sync-engine extraction) stay post-launch.

## PRIOR (2026-07-22 — SESSION 309: **THE STEP-BACK + THE LABEL LAW** — Captain's ownership mandate: a 6-lane refute-verified Workflow fleet + a keeper own-eyes walk of the full 17-step tour on real gestures, then the label ruling and a stack of finishing bouts; gates: typecheck 0 + **1416 vitest** + **256 e2e, PLAYWRIGHT_EXIT=0** + builds clean; UNCOMMITTED; full record `Notes/2026-07-22-s309-stepback.md`)

- **THE OWNERSHIP STEP-BACK.** Six lanes (queue-verify, engineering-map, design-completeness, day2-product, show-drift, test-health; the three judgment lanes refute-verified by opus skeptics, ~950K tokens) ran beside a keeper walk of every tour step with real clicks, real Tab, real zone-polygon click (`Notes/ui-snapshots/2026-07-22-s309/`). **Verdict: strong, no release blocker** — the tour runs end-to-end, every hands-on beat behaves, the four-family palette reads on the live sentence. **Queue-verify killed five rotted lines** (em-dash sweep already done S308 · pin-drag beat done S299 · keep-the-world cut by Captain S299 · zones-on-share-map + tour-resilience done S299) and confirmed the rest with file:line evidence. **Day-2 lens** found recency RECORDED AND NEVER READ (`registry.ts:305-306` writes `active_world_id`/`last_opened_at`, zero readers — bare-URL return lands on welcome, not last world; narrowed low/med since the worlds list doubles as the FS re-grant gesture) and global search keyboard-only (Cmd+K palette is the only cross-room search, no visible affordance; `room-nav.ts:52` hints deliberate). **Test-health** found `room-nav.ts` (268 lines) at ZERO coverage — the exact file S308 found two live bugs in — and `tour.spec.ts` driving steps by hand-maintained numeric index at 20+ sites, the same string/index coupling class that shipped the preweb dead gate.
- **THE LABEL LAW — Captain ruled: creating a link NEVER changes the prose text, any match kind, ever.** Before this, alias matches resolved the writer's visible word to the canonical name (link-all rewrote the flagship tour sentence in four places — "The Hegemony The Consul sat…"). Now `promotionLabel` in `core/prose-autolink.ts` is the ONE rule site; the live Tab handler delegates to it. The chip-label rename sync now folds case via the shared `isValidChipLabel` — **it was case-sensitive and would have undone the rule one tick after promotion** (and would have undone even the S307 GUARANTEE). Guarded by: THE GUARANTEE alias sweep extended, a link-then-unlink identity property, `isValidChipLabel` units, and a byte-identical prose assertion across link-all in the tour walk e2e. Revert-checked (old rule → 4 loud failures). Own-eyes verified live: text unchanged, labels = writer's words, ids = canonical elements, persisted markdown clean. DESIGN_WRITING convention **#49b**.
- **THE BOUTS, all gated:** **search pill** in the room-nav strip, anchored right, field-look — opens the SAME Cmd+K palette (one search, two doors; Captain's design), e2e-locked in command-palette.spec · **linking step scrolls the World section into view** on step entry (measured live: everything the step pointed at was below the dock at y=572; new `data-section` attr is the stable hook on detail sections; revert-checked) · **curly-apostrophe sweep** across the tour sentence, bundle names/aliases/story, refs and tests in five files (refs match names verbatim, so not one char) · **`installRowKeydownActivation`** shared helper in dom-utils wired into the elements list, worlds list, and map tree — Enter/Space now activate the rows their role promises · **room-nav preview buttons** aria-disabled + teaching tooltips (native `disabled` had killed hover) · **welcome-panel teaching** fires on focus/blur too + open-folder got its missing DETAILS entry · **demo counts DERIVED from the payload** (copy + both e2e asserts; three unlinked hardcodes → one source) · the last `##/w/` href (sticky bar) · **Esc in the Data room** no longer destroys a pasted payload (`/import` joins the owned-surface gate) · **two folder buttons capability-gated** (demo folder-CTA + settings folder button, instances 2+3 of the class) · **element footer** uses relativeTime with the exact stamp in the tooltip · **share list** shows an "edited" badge when content drifted since publish.
- **HOUSEKEEPING (Captain's mid-session ask):** cos-browser phantom "modified" gitlink untracked + gitignored in Forge (one staged deletion shows until next commit, then gone). atlas `Notes/ui-snapshots/` untracked + gitignored in the atlas repo (files stay on disk); the screenshot regime is written into /quench step 8 as a per-session sweep.
- **DECISIONS CLOSED (Captain, 2026-07-22):** repo stays private (no Notes/PROGRESS strip needed) · boot-resume dropped (deep-link resume covers his path) · Simmons line kept · writing-room delete IS in scope (banked) · palette four-families pending his eyeball only.
- ⚡ **NEXT** (specs in the stepback note): **Bout W — writing-room delete** (Captain: in scope; investigate the chain first — children of a deleted Book/Chapter, propose children stay + become top-level, count named in the confirm) · **Bout C — guards** (tour.spec step-id-matches-`TOUR_STEPS[N-1]` assertion · room-nav first unit test · `elementMatchesQuery` units) · **Bout D — opportunities** (story-tier search, MEASURE perf on the 588-element world first · knowledge link-all corner) · **og:image** pick (two 1200×630 candidates in `Notes/ui-snapshots/2026-07-22-s309/`) · Captain: the copy pass, the push (**UNCOMMITTED** on the S308 pile — a push, not a commit, unblocks Boss; **`tools/show` needs its own redeploy too**) · Skeld: `#009a00` verdict LANDED same-day — FAILS CVD (ΔE 0.2 vs Temporal amber, protan), REVERTED to `#008300`; the World-recession trade is held for Captain (record in `core/element-colors.ts` bottom comment; Skeld recommends keep #008300).

## PRIOR (2026-07-22 — SESSION 308: **THE RELEASE SWEEP** — a nine-lane refute-verified audit + own-eyes pass, then Bout 0, the palette collapse onto FOUR FAMILIES, an accessibility boundary, and Captain's round; gates: typecheck 0 + **1411 vitest** + **255 e2e, PLAYWRIGHT_EXIT=0** + atlas & show builds clean; UNCOMMITTED; findings sheet `Notes/2026-07-22-s308-release-sweep.md`)

- **A DEAD GATE HAD BEEN SHIPPING SINCE S306c.** `tour-bar.ts` looked up `atlas-tour-preweb` by the step id `'where-it-lives'` — merged into `yours-to-keep` three sessions ago. `findIndex` returned **-1**, so `idx < -1` was false for every step, the class never applied, and `atlas.css:2016` never matched: **the Web tab has been visible from step 5 of 17 for the tour's whole post-S306 life**, while `linking` deliberately withholds its name ("naming a tab the user cannot see yet is a promise the chrome does not keep") and `yours-to-keep` announces it as arriving. A renamed step id silently disarms any gate keyed on it. The e2e only ever asserted the PRESENT half; the ABSENT half is now the pair, revert-checked (`Expected: hidden, Received: visible`).
- **A NAMELESS ELEMENT PERMANENTLY BROKE THE SIDEBAR.** `elements-sidebar.ts` sorted on a bare `a.name.localeCompare(b.name)` — the ONE sort site in the codebase that skipped the guard every sibling uses. `name` is typed non-optional, which is exactly why it shipped: a type is a compile-time claim about data the compiler controls, and element JSON is data it does not. Guarded, and the guard is a **source scan over all of src/** (`name-sort-guarded.test.ts`), not a test of the one line — **it flagged two more sites on its first run** (false positives in the palette, guarded anyway rather than allowlisted: a text scan cannot tell an Element from a PaletteResult, and an invariant with exceptions is one the next reader must re-derive).
- **THE PUSH PATH HAD NO try/catch WHERE THE PULL PATH HAS ONE** — with a comment saying "we MUST restore idle state first or the pill stays stuck forever". Any throw in `runPushOnly` left the pill reading "Saving…" indefinitely, on the path every user edit takes. Mirrored, revert-checked (`expected 'syncing' to be 'idle'`). The instrument was wrong first: injecting at `listElements` made the round SUCCEED, because `pushPhase` never calls it.
- **THE PALETTE COLLAPSED ONTO FOUR FAMILIES** (`core/element-colors.ts`). Atlas shipped TWO unrelated 22-hue maps, visible at once in the three-pane view. Skeld's CVD validator refuted BOTH, and my own recommendation with them: Character vs Creature ΔE 6.3 against a floor of 15; **Title vs Trait ΔE 1.3 for deutan viewers**; Object and Construct below the chroma floor. Past ~4 hues mutual separation is unachievable in the hard case, so colour now carries FAMILY and the icon carries TYPE. Built in the SDK's proposed shape (`ELEMENT_FAMILIES` / `FAMILY_COLORS` / `elementColor`) so the post-launch swap is a re-export. **Atlas-side evidence that reshaped the ruling**: `map-tab-pins.ts:59` colours a pin by its TARGET element's type, so the Spatial hues almost never render on a map at all — the fold into World is right for a different reason than modelled.
- **A LUMINANCE FINDING THE VALIDATOR COULD NOT PRODUCE**: the four hues are validated for pair separation, not for parity against the surface. Against `#0a0a0a`, Temporal reads 6.84:1, Agents 5.77, Abstract 5.32 and **World 4.25** — a 1.61x spread with the floor on the family covering Location/Object/Construct, so locations recede while events come forward. `#009a00` measures 5.62 and drops the spread to 1.29x; running it as a PROPOSAL pending Skeld's re-validation, one character to revert.
- **ICON LIGATURES WERE THE ACCESSIBLE NAME.** Read off the live a11y tree, controls announced as `"add cloud_upload"`, `"wall_art pages"`, `"import_export import"`. 253 render sites, six `aria-hidden` in the whole codebase, and no shared icon helper to fix at — so `ui/icon-a11y.ts` creates the boundary as one observer, with a stated demolition date. **The sweep then created its own gap and its own audit caught it**: the type-filter buttons carry `data-tip`, not a label, so hiding the ligature made them silent rather than wrong. `aria-label` added at the render site; 84 icons, zero unstamped, zero unnamed controls.
- **THE DATA ROOM WAS BRANDED EVERYWHERE EXCEPT WHERE IT IS PRINTED**: a stale label-shortener in both `room-nav` render paths and a palette entry keyed on the route segment meant the tooltip said "Data" while the label said "import", and **searching "data" in Cmd+K found nothing**. Aliases added so "import"/"export" still match but "data" is what displays.
- **CAPTAIN'S ROUND 2**: **"more information" anchored** — the deeper bulb flowed right after the body, so its Y moved with copy length; the docked copy now fills the panel as a column and pins its footer to the bottom, so the bulb lands at a constant 89-91px from the dock bottom on every step (measured 2/3/4/6). **Tour TRUTH fixes** (taste left for Captain's pass): Possessions/Affiliations named sections the app renders as Objects/Institutions under a World heading (3 of 4 things the payoff step pointed at were mis-named — verified against `ELEMENT_SECTIONS.character`), "Referenced-by" → "Referenced by", "all linked to the swamps" was false for 2 of 4 and schema-impossible for 1, and `unlocks:['rightpane']` moved from the-planet to the-map so the pane opens WITH the map instead of a step before the narration about it (e2e now a present/absent pair). **Skipper screen**: the zero-worlds pane names the next action + points at the create control, and the "start here" highlight is now persistent while zero worlds exist instead of dismissed-forever-on-hover. **Docs truth pass**: DESIGN_IMPORT header, four dead DESIGN_PUBLISHING links, README Chromium-only clause, SYNC_NOTES first-page-data claim — all corrected.
- **CAPTAIN'S ROUND 1**: zone labels always-on for published pages (payload already carried the name; only the hover `<title>` used it) · the share-page zone click artifact traced — `role=button` + `tabindex=0` on an SVG path with no focus style, so Chromium drew its UA focus ring as a black-and-white blob; fixed with `:focus-visible` so the keyboard ring the tabindex exists for survives · "element card" → "element" (LABEL only; `kind: 'element-card'` is a persisted wire id and `compilePageStory` is a derived artifact pushed to the server — renaming it would churn every page's story) · Exit removed from the opener. **Both zone fixes RE-LIFTED into `tools/show`** (its render.ts is a deliberate copy) — needs a redeploy to reach published pages.
- **THE OPENER'S EXIT REMOVAL WOULD HAVE TRAPPED PEOPLE.** Captain asked for one control; doing exactly that left no way off the cover screen for anyone who clicked "take the tour" to see what it was. Escape now opens the exit confirm **on the opener only** — deliberately not global, because S307 found a globally-captured Escape in zone edit that reverted every committed vertex drag. The R3 relaunch spec now proves the ESCAPE ROUTE survives unmount/remount, which is stronger than the button assertion it replaces.
- **THE SESSION'S THEME — five instances of a decision applied to ONE surface**: the S301 `aria-disabled` fix never reached the room nav · S300's count>0 filter strip lives only inside the tour · S306's link-all button never reached the knowledge editor · the Data rename skipped the nav label and Cmd+K · `runPullOnly` got its try/catch and `runPushOnly` did not. ## PRIOR (2026-07-21 — SESSION 307: **THE STRANDING SWEEP** — a five-lane read-only audit, then the fixes: the replay bug, pin uniqueness, zone-edit data loss + first tests, autolink case, and a tour step whose view was a side effect; gates: typecheck 0 + **1405 vitest** + **255 e2e, PLAYWRIGHT_EXIT=0, zero flaky across two consecutive full runs** + build clean; UNCOMMITTED; full record `Notes/2026-07-21-s307-handover.md`)

- **THE REPLAY BUG WAS A START BUG, NOT A TEARDOWN BUG.** `startTour` only ever ran from a first-launch landing, so it ASSUMED a bare stage instead of establishing one. Replaying from settings left three things behind, each revert-checked separately: the launching ROUTE (nothing in the start path navigates, and `routeFor` is gated on a worldId that is `''` until the create beat), the user's WORLD (open, and still syncing, under the dock), and — **the blocker** — `worlds-sidebar` tested `if (root)` BEFORE the tour branch while `isTouringNoRoot()` required `!atlasRoot`. Finishing the tour is exactly what sets a root, so every replayer took the root branch, whose create button carries no tour override, while `tour-bar` gates the step on `tourWorldExists()`. **No Next, no scripted create: hard stuck at step 1** — and pressing the live +folder built a REAL world in their own folder and left them stuck anyway. `clearStageForTour()` now makes the stage; the tour branch wins the sidebar; the world list shows demo worlds only.
- **THE TEST THAT HAD NEVER EXISTED**: no tour spec ever started with `state.active !== null` or `atlasRoot !== null` — every one begins `freshBoot()` and clicks the landing button, and `grep "replay-tour" tests/` returned nothing. That gap is why a hard-stuck path shipped green. The new walk establishes both preconditions and is revert-checked against all three halves independently.
- **PIN UNIQUENESS, one wall at the funnel**: guarded in `createElement` gated on `type === 'pin'`, NOT in `createPin` — the import applier calls `createElement('pin', ...)` directly, so a guard in the wrapper would have looked right and silently missed import. The rule lives in `core/pin-uniqueness.ts` (pure predicate + typed `DuplicatePinError`) and MockFolderStore mirrors it from the SAME predicate (convention 20). Four cascade fixtures re-pointed at a SECOND MAP rather than a second character — an element on two maps is the truer shape, and it tests a cross-map sweep a same-map pair never did.
- **ZONE EDIT: the data-loss trio, plus the first tests this 1000-line module has ever had.** `abortEditModeForMap` nulled the session and orphaned the persisted snapshot — and its caller runs on **world switch**, so: edit a zone, drag two vertices (each autosaved to disk), switch worlds, come back, and `recoverFromOrphanSnapshot` REVERTED both drags while calling them "unsaved". There is no undo anywhere in atlas. `exitEditMode` cleared the snapshot under the CURRENT world while its own comment claimed it used the session's — `EditSession` had no `worldId` field at all, so it structurally could not do what it said. And the snapshot was deleted while the restore it protects was still in flight. Also: Enter/Escape were captured globally with no target check, so dismissing a field in the zone's still-mounted detail panel reverted every vertex drag.
- **ZONE KERNELS** (`zone-edit-kernels.ts`, pure, store-free): `computeInsertion` renumbers by POSITION — the old code used an array INDEX as an `order` VALUE, so with non-contiguous orders a click on segment 1 inserted on segment 0 (visibly self-intersecting polygon), and on an IMPORTED zone where every order is absent it always appended regardless of which edge was clicked. `commitVertexPosition` clamps to the canvas, matching `scaleSpatialPatches`; the drag path clamped nothing, so an out-of-bounds vertex survived until an image attach rescaled-and-clamped it and deformed the shape (the S300 corruption class, through the path with no test).
- **AUTOLINK IS NOW CASE-INSENSITIVE, AND THAT IS SAFE ONLY BECAUSE IT IS CASE-PRESERVING.** Captain's ask, and the two halves are ONE decision: the index folds its keys and the scanner carries `i`, while every promotion path labels the chip with the writer's own matched text. Folding without preserving would have turned every lowercase mention into a silent recapitalisation of the prose. Guarded by "THE GUARANTEE" — a sweep of four casings through BOTH engines (markdown + richtext). A genuinely different alias ("the duchess" to "Aralia") still resolves to the canonical name: a separately tested, deliberate decision, left alone and now stated explicitly as the paired half. Regex flags are read from `index.scanner.flags` at every rebuild site, because two of them restated `'g'` and would have silently dropped the `i`.
- **TAB LINKS, IT DOES NOT ESCAPE.** With the cursor off a dotted word the handler returned false, handing Tab to the browser, which moves focus to the next control — the link-all corner button sits exactly there, which is what Captain hit twice. Tab now jumps to the next hint and promotes it (repeated Tab walks the chapter), and only moves focus when there is nothing left to link.
- **A STEP'S VIEW IS A FUNCTION OF THE STEP.** `the-consul` carried NO route: going forward it looked correct only because creating an element navigates to it as a SIDE EFFECT, so going BACK the middle pane kept showing a later step's element under the Consul's narration. Now routed — and `routeFor` falls back to world home when the ref is not ON DISK. **Own-eyes caught the trap inside my own fix**: `resolveRef` resolves against the BUNDLE, so it succeeds for every scripted element whether revealed or not, and the first cut sent the hands-on beat to a raw uuid reading "not found in this world" — worse than the bug it replaced, with all 18 tour e2e green, because none of them assert what the middle pane SHOWS.
- **THE SENTENCE GAPS WERE A HALF-APPLIED DECISION.** S306c made the opener render bare and accepted the reflow ("the shift hides inside the dock transition"), but left every LATER step emitting unlit glyphs at `opacity: 0.05` with their width reserved — which is what spaced the words apart for the whole rest of the tour. Unlit glyphs are no longer rendered at all.
- **CHARTS BAR**: the calendar chip is PINNED, built before the type chips. Minting a calendar creates a Construct, which adds a type chip, which used to shove the calendar chip sideways — its position was a function of world CONTENTS, so the act of setting a calendar moved its own control. Type chips now live in their own wrapper so a growing set cannot displace the pinned controls. The now control and the view toggles are on one line, toggles first: the fixed pair leads, the control that changes shape trails.
- **SMALLER, ALL VERIFIED FIRST**: "Delete + recreate to recover" replaced (the files were never at risk; the one-click fix was in the same header) · the empty-world element pane no longer instructs you to pick from a list that renders nothing, and a not-found id gets a way back · `sync-toasts`' `local-only` branch now navigates to the fix like `pin-missing` always has, and the dead `attached-but-paused` variant (declared, handled, never CONSTRUCTED) was deleted · the finale copy no longer promises a folder picker that OPFS-only browsers never get · `reorderSiblings` no longer fails silently mid-loop · the maps step explains where a map image comes from (URL or a local file kept in the world folder, never uploaded) · the exit-modal footnote removed.
- **AN AGENT CLAIM I REFUTED**: the rot sweep reported `data-tour-control` as queried-but-never-set (gate R14 dead). Four elements set it — the grep had missed `dataset.tourControl` in camelCase. The guard is live; nothing was deleted. Agents are evidence, not oracles.
- ⚡ **NEXT**: the orphan-zone geometry dead end (the ONLY live instance of "an existing element cannot reach its constructor flow" — map got its fix in S253, pin and marker have no exists-but-empty state, so this is isolated rather than the edge of a pattern) · the zero-worlds pane CTA (Captain's S255v2 call, a design decision not a defect) · `applyChart`'s one-way calendar ratchet · the reverted chart-framing fix (spec in the S305 addendum) · 429 has no branch in the make-online error ladder · a partial push reports a failure COUNT but never WHICH elements, though the per-slot records already exist. Held for Captain: the copy pass, the push.

## PRIOR (2026-07-21 — SESSION 306: **THE WRITING BEAT (the tour's last unbuilt piece) + a five-agent survey + six banked fixes**; gates: typecheck 0 + **1371 vitest** + **full e2e 249 passed, PLAYWRIGHT_EXIT=0 with 2 flaky walks**; **S301-S305 are COMMITTED and 5 commits AHEAD OF ORIGIN, UNPUSHED**; S306 uncommitted on top; full record `Notes/2026-07-21-s306-handover.md`)

- **THE "UNCOMMITTED PILE" LINE WAS STALE.** Captain committed S301-S305 as "tour rework part 1-5". The working tree was clean at session start. What blocks a Boss redeploy is a **`git push`**, not a commit.
- **THE WRITING BEAT SHIPPED, and not as any handover imagined it.** Captain rejected my first design on COHERENCE grounds: the tour's spine is THE SENTENCE, so spending its one hands-on writing moment on the unrelated `Story of the old piano` breaks the through-line. The landing shape: **Chapter 1 is baked as PLAIN prose** (all 9 chips stripped) and the user links the sentence they have been building all tour, with autolink's **Tab-on-hover**. One step, no step-count growth.
  - **The tier choice was CUT as unreachable**, not as a preference: `isWritingInitialized` correctly suppresses the setup screen when every chain member's supertype matches a writing label, which the bundle's Book/Chapter do. Showing it would need a world that misrepresents itself. Tiers are taught by SHOWING three real ones in the tree instead.
  - **The selection bubble was dropped as unnecessary** — the gesture is Tab-on-hover with right-click as the alternate, both of which the writing room already had. It remains a real standalone gap (share has it, writing does not) and is banked.
  - **SEVEN of nine phrases are linkable at that beat**, because Rachmaninov and his Prelude arrive at `deep-time` three steps later. Phase c says so instead of promising a fully linked sentence. `TOUR_WRITING_LINKABLE = 7`, recomputed by a unit test from the bundle + reveal order.
- **THE BUNDLE FIX THAT MATTERED**: the character `Steinway` collided with the `Steinway` alias on `The Consul's Steinway`, and autolink/bulk-link skip any key with 2+ candidates — **that word could never have linked, silently**. Renamed to `Mr. Steinway`. Also: `Story of the old piano` gained `supertype: 'Draft'` (without a writing-label supertype it was INVISIBLE in the writing room). Both mirrored to the LIVE world with Captain's write key, verified by independent re-read.
- **SIX BANKED FIXES SWEPT**: the Web tab named 5 steps early · `set-now` failing silently (`void setNow(...)` at every call site; that write IS the tour's forward gate) · **chart delete had no confirm at all** (the only unguarded destructive action on a user-authored artifact; no undo exists anywhere in atlas) + a decline-side test so the guard cannot be silently removed · **home and Charts disagreed on the demo world's dates** (home froze with no `calendarId`; first-run visible) · **Maps had no help entry at all**, so `?` on a map opened nothing.
- **THE NUL BYTE**: `core/calendar.ts` used a raw `'\x00'` sentinel, which makes ripgrep treat the file as BINARY — every content search over 704 lines of calendar logic silently returned nothing, while `--count` still returned a plausible number. Replaced with a private-use sentinel. **Grep reads the file now.**
- **TWO OF MY OWN BANKED CLAIMS WERE WRONG** (a queue line is a claim, and claims rot): "a failed set-now is silent" was real but "a class issue across every single-route beat" was FALSE — every other hands-on beat toasts or falls through gracefully; and "`fallbackToOnly` has no non-tour test" was ALREADY FIXED (`calendar-lens.test.ts` has four dedicated cases).
- **KNOWN FLAKE (named, not hidden)**: the two long tour walks fail-then-heal on retry. `handleKeyDown` promotes the decoration containing the CURSOR, so Tab only lands once the click's selection is applied; waiting on `ProseMirror-focused` resolves from a cached snapshot without a round-trip and is NOT sufficient. Helper waits on the selection sitting inside a hint and retries the pair. Fix if it annoys: give the WALK tests a deterministic path (the `movePinToSwamps` precedent) and keep the real gesture in the dedicated test.
- **THE PALETTE LIFT (S306b, close-gated, 2 opus lenses)**: `roomOf` + the desk click-through moved to **`src/core/element-room.ts`**; the palette AND the elements sidebar now route through it, so Cmd+K on a map/page/knowledge entry/chapter no longer dumps you in the generic field editor. Same shape and reason as `core/element-search.ts` (S258). **The gate earned its keep three times**: (1) a HIGH **confirmed by a RUN, not a source-read** — `openMapTab` never navigates, so from the palette (global; fires inside the writing editor where focus/reading mode hides the right pane) a map jump was a DEAD CLICK, the exact failure the function exists to prevent; (2) my first fix (always navigate) broke a SHIPPED home behaviour, caught by the existing S265 re-activation spec, so routing is now an explicit `routeToMap` option with both reasons written down; (3) the fix was **INCOMPLETE** — the palette's own "see all" handoff target, the elements sidebar, still used a bare hash, so one query gave two destinations. **Map rows in the SIDEBAR deliberately still open the element page** (that is how you reach Image URL, the real S300 sketch-rescale path — an existing spec caught the regression). +7 unit (`element-room.test.ts`) +2 e2e, all revert-checked.
- **S306c — CAPTAIN'S SMOKE ROUND (pushed)**: the opener renders the sentence **BARE** (no glyph slots on the first screen — they arrive with the dock, where the layout changes anyway so the reflow hides in the transition); grey book cite cut; **Begin → Start**; sentence colours one word per element (The=title, Hegemony=institution, Consul keeps character — a title span on word 2 was overwriting him); the consul step acknowledges its own create instead of still asking; the false "the zone you traced onto the map" corrected (the zone is REVEALED, the user never draws it); Consul aliases trimmed to `['Consul']`; **`where-it-lives` + `online` MERGED into one `yours-to-keep` close → 17 steps**; map delete closes its right-pane tab; home "a page of your world" → "part of your world".

Instrumenting the real gesture showed focus landing on `atlas-writing-linkall-corner` exactly as he guessed. Mechanism: `handleKeyDown` promotes the decoration containing the **CURSOR**, and my copy said "hover a dotted word" — hovering places no cursor, so the handler correctly returns false and the browser moves focus. Copy now says **click inside** a dotted word. **The better find underneath**: the prose panel already carries a **link-all corner button with a live count** (`refreshLinkAllWidget`) — phase b had been sending people to a right-click tree menu when there is a button in the page they are looking at.

The two long WALKS now click the link-all corner (a real user gesture, and the one the copy recommends) while the **dedicated writing-beat test keeps click+Tab** as the precise thing under test — the `movePinToSwamps` precedent: the walk's subject is the walk, not the keystroke. `tour.spec.ts` went 17/17 with retries OFF and ~2.5min → ~48s.

The Consul's tenure is a **sourced eleven years** (two independent lines) — a better number than our invented grant-at-3. The Steinway is **"Pre-Hegira"** and the Consul **requested it** on his ship; **Rachmaninov is named exactly once** (the opening sentence) and never dated. A single line (L1130) reconciles four calendars and yields P.C. epoch ≈ AD 2143 — but "P.C." appears **once in the whole book**, so it is the thinnest possible evidence. ⚠ **THE OPENING SCENE IS NOT ON HYPERION** (keeper-verified at L307: an unnamed world, "solitude he had earned through the pain and nightmare he had already suffered on Hyperion"); our bundle fuses them. Captain's call: solve with the unknown world next session.

## PRIOR (2026-07-21 — SESSION 305 + CLOSE GATE: **the charts act, the exit confirm, a calendar default; the framing fix REVERTED by its own gate**; gates: typecheck 0 + **1367/1367 vitest** + **full e2e 249/249, exit 0 direct, quiet machine**; UNCOMMITTED; full record `Notes/2026-07-21-s305-handover.md` + its ADDENDUM)

- **THE FRAMING FIX IS REVERTED.** A 3-lens close gate found two BLOCKERs, both demonstrated with real numbers: (1) `coverageIntervals` read `endDate` raw and never `effectiveEndDate`, so an OPEN-ENDED node (a title granted and never revoked) was a POINT to the algorithm and got trimmed as an outlier while RENDERING as a bar across the very gap it called empty — and **my own docstring claimed that case was handled**, while the test I wrote for it used a RECORDED end and exercised a different path entirely; (2) `extendRangeForOpenEnds` iterates ALL nodes including trimmed ones, so a discarded open-ended node dragged `max` back out and produced a window WIDER than the plain fit (measured span 5101 vs 4160) — the smear fix making the smear worse. Also: the focused element could be framed off-screen, LOD rungs downgraded at the framed view, `total` went stale across passes, and one "no-op" test passed vacuously. Reverted rather than rushed at low context; the handover carries a full spec for redoing it properly.
- **THE EXIT CONFIRM SHIPPED** (Captain's own S301 idea): leaving mid-tour opens a small modal listing the rooms the tour never reached, one blurb each, then confirms. Pure kernel `src/tour/tour-exit-summary.ts` (store-free, DOM-free, 8 unit tests) + `tour-exit-modal.ts`. A room counts as SEEN only once a step ROUTED there — unlocking an icon is a promise, not a visit. The finale's Finish deliberately does NOT route through it. By the finale the untouched rooms are exactly Knowledge, Pages and Data, asserted so a future beat in one fails loudly.
- **THREE GATE FINDINGS FIXED IN MY OWN WORK**: a **vacuous invariant** (the "every hands-on kind has a forward gate" test compared the closed union against a hardcoded copy of itself in the same file — deleting the `set-now` branch from the bar left it green while the step became permanently un-advanceable; it now reads `tour-bar.ts` and is revert-checked) · **`deep-time`'s reveal contents were unlocked** (ordering was tested, contents were not, so deleting the reveal list kept the suite green) · a **false rationale repeated in four places** (I claimed the document view has no now control; it does — the control bar is a SIBLING of the view mount, and the scroll view even reads an open-ended span as "3 PC → now" of its own accord).
- **GATE CLAIMS THAT REFUTED MY OWN BRIEFS**: the title's bar spans essentially the whole visible axis (`time_range_min` is never read by the axis), so that copy under-promises rather than over-promises; and the `undefined`-vs-`null` calendar distinction survives localStorage intact, so the menu's explicit "no calendar" is holdable.
- **BANKED from the gate**: world home and the Charts room disagree on the demo world (home builds a synthetic chart with no `calendarId` and freezes strictly) · `applyChart` writes an explicit `null` and is a one-way ratchet out of the new calendar default · a failed `set-now` is silent, a class issue across every single-route beat · clearing `now` bounces the narrator back to phase a.

- **THE "LOST" NOTES WERE NEVER LOST (my error, corrected)**: I searched Forge's `Notes/` for the S301/S302 tour notes, found nothing, and told Captain they had never been committed. Atlas graduated into its OWN repo — its notes live in `tools/atlas/Notes/` and its docs cite them atlas-relative. All eight exist and are committed. Read atlas doc paths as atlas-relative.
- **CAPTAIN'S RULINGS**: the two events share ONE date (the sentence has him playing WHILE they surge) · the event-vs-narrative BRANCH is CUT in favour of a "more information" note, which removes the reveal-slot kernel change and its gate entirely · Rachmaninov's depth is mine to choose, with no Earth-to-PC conversion ever asserted · a wide span with a tight cluster must not smear, as real app work.

Now needs >= 6 dated nodes and a 0.75 kept share; merged coverage intervals let a long-lived element BRIDGE the gap it crosses. Revert-checked.

Keys on `undefined` (never chosen), never falsiness: `null` is an explicit "no calendar" and must survive. Opt-in so a saved chart with no lens never adopts one minted later.
- **THE CHARTS ACT** (17 → 18 steps): `the-events` (both events, one date) · `the-chart` = **the now beat** (`handsOn: 'set-now'`, single route; the bundle ships no `time_current` so the real button is pressable; `atlas-tour-nowstep` pulses it and HIDES the canvas/document toggle, which has no now control at all; phase b narrates the gold line and the title's open-ended span) · `deep-time` (NEW: Rachmaninov + Prelude in the Old Earth era).

A real click on the swamps polygon also placed the pin in-zone, re-confirming the S304 fall-through.

## PRIOR STATE (2026-07-21 — SESSION 304: **THE ACT RESTRUCTURE — step-0 opener, maps before objects, institution+title merged, the pin's SOFT correction, a zone-click bug the reorder surfaced; close-gated**; gates: typecheck 0 + **1344/1344 vitest** + **full e2e 248/248, exit 0 direct, quiet machine** (the documented speed-bout park/unpark flaky did not trip this run) + build clean; UNCOMMITTED on top of the S302/S303 pile)

Captain's afternoon mandate: "take the work, aim high, get ready for a proper full autonomous bout... work until 60-70% context." This is the next bout named in every handover — unblocked because S303 refactored staging to capability classes. The restructure is Captain's dump of 2026-07-20, this act of it built and gated.

- **THE STEP-0 OPENER (new, S304)**: the tour opens on the Hyperion sentence CENTRE-STAGE at vignette scale on black (`the-sentence` step, no `unlocks` → `body.atlas-touring:not(.atlas-tour-cap-shell)` hides the shell). The bar renders `.is-opener` (screen-centered, auto height, 22px sentence) and a single **Begin** that hands off into the docked narrator (the shell materializes on the create-world beat). Begin is a bar special-case: `canAdvance` is false with no active world, so the opener is pre-world, not suspended. Own-eyes verified (`Notes/ui-snapshots/2026-07-21/`). Copy is Captain's shorter variant, provisional, rides his pass.
- **THE REORDER**: maps (planet · map+zone · pin · swamps' living things) now come BEFORE the objects run and linking, so the pin's **"sat"** (word 3) lights in reading order ahead of "balcony"/"spaceship". The institution and title MERGED into one step (a title is a name of the institution beat; both reveal, the copy teaches the distinction). The Swamps zone + its 9 markers now reveal WITH the map (idx 5) instead of at the swamps step — the pin's soft correction needs the region drawn to test the drop against. 17 steps, `data-tour-step` 1-17. Three unit invariants (caps grow, every unlock known, tour reaches all caps) + a new "zone revealed no later than its place-pin step" invariant guard the order.
- **THE PIN SOFT CORRECTION (Captain's ruling: never refuse a placement)**: `TourStep.placeInZone: {pinRef, zoneRef}` + `TourWorldView.pinInZone` (the zone polygon from its markers × `ZoneGeometry.pointInPolygon`). Place anywhere → the pin lands (locked identity); Next appears only once it sits over the swamps. A drop outside shows phase **b** ("drag the pin onto the swamps", no Next); dragging it in resolves phase **c** and opens Next. The phase `when`s are PREFIX-nested (b `hasRef(pin)`, c `hasRef(pin) && pinInZone`) so `resolvePhase` never breaks early. The +pin highlight releases when the pin EXISTS (the gesture is done); the correction is a drag. Watch-mode/resume mint the pin at baked coords, which sit inside the zone — locked by a bundle-geometry unit test so a re-bake that moves either out of overlap fails loudly. Verified end-to-end with own-eyes (place on the zone → phase c → Next).
- **A ZONE-CLICK BUG THE REORDER SURFACED (app-wide fix)**: a zone polygon is `interactive` and `stopPropagation`s clicks (to select the zone). With the zone now drawn during the pin beat, clicking the swamps to place the pin SELECTED the region instead — the beat could not be completed. `ZoneOverlay.setClickFallsThrough(() => !!ui.pinPlacement)` (wired in map-tab.ts): while placing a pin, a zone click falls THROUGH to the map's placement handler and does not select the zone. Correct app-wide, not just the tour. Own-eyes verified (clicked the zone directly → pin placed inside it at storage 198,302, consul stayed selected). Found by walking the promise through the stack — no test imagined it.
- **THE E2E RACE, caught by own-eyes**: the first full-walk run failed with "no pin". The probe (dev server + Playwright, my own eyes) proved the placement works on AND off the zone — the failure was a race: `.click()` resolves before the app's async `createPin` lands, so the test's coordinate-nudge found no pin. Fixed with a poll for the pin before nudging. The pixel→storage mapping of a raw click is not what the specs assert (the pinInZone GATE is; the geometry is unit-tested), so the specs force the pin to the zone centroid via the store.
- **FULL E2E REWRITE**: tour.spec renumbered to the new order (opener + Begin hand-off asserted, the merged hegemony step, maps-before-objects, the pin soft correction + the click-directly-on-the-zone gesture each as their own test). 15 tour e2e, all green. +11 vitest (pinInZone synthetic geometry incl. order-sensitivity + foreign-zone markers + the map_id scoping; the baked-pin-in-swamps guard; the zone-reveal-order invariant).
- **THE CLOSE GATE (3 opus lenses over the diff, ~428K)**: 1 HIGH + 1 MED + 4 LOW. **The HIGH was REFUTED by the real-run** — a lens read Leaflet source (with line numbers) and concluded a zone polygon on a plain LayerGroup can't propagate a click to the map, so the fall-through couldn't place a pin on the zone. A REAL Playwright click on the zone polygon placed the pin in-zone, left the Consul selected, and opened Next — the source-read was wrong, the run is the oracle (agents are evidence, not oracles). But the gate correctly caught that **no e2e exercised the click-ON-zone path** (the other pin specs click off-zone and force coords) — added one that clicks the polygon directly. **The MED was real and fixed**: double-clicking the zone during the pin beat entered zone EDIT mode (the dblclick handler had no placement guard) and trapped placement behind edit-mode's map-click handling, with live vertex handles that could reshape the very polygon pinInZone tests against — now `onZoneDoubleClick` early-returns when placement is armed. LOWs fixed: `pinInZone` now filters markers by `map_id` like the renderer (a multi-map zone can't corrupt the polygon); dead `TOUR_ACT_NAMES` deleted (its premise broke — act:1 objects/linking now sit after act:2 maps); clicking the swamps BEFORE arming +pin used to select the zone and route off the Consul (hiding the +pin button) — during the pin beat (`atlas-tour-pinstep`) a zone click now falls through to a harmless no-op, e2e-locked. One LOW banked for Captain's copy pass: the linking "more information" copy names the Web tab five steps before it appears.
- **THE OWN-EYES PROXY LIE (kept)**: my first "verification" that clicking the zone places a pin used synthetic `dispatchEvent(MouseEvent)`, which does NOT reproduce Leaflet's real click delegation — it likely placed via the map-background path, a convenient proxy that behaved differently from the user's click. The gate's HIGH forced a REAL Playwright click, which settled it (the feature works). Verify in the user's real input space, not a synthetic proxy — the law bit even while I was applying it.
- ⚡ **NEXT** (from the same dump, still to build): the WRITING beat (tier choice + link-three-words + the selection bubble wired into the writing room, share-only today) · the CHARTS act element-by-element (HELD for Captain's look; controls need `data-action` hooks; the event-vs-narrative branch needs the reveal/heal kernel to treat two bundle entries as one slot — Captain greenlit, GATE before build) · the two-era Old Earth/PC calendar baked into the bundle · the EXIT MODAL (rooms seen/unseen). Held for Captain: the copy pass over every phase body (the ~90px dock slack), step-0 copy, the charts-step look, the council motions. Cross-bay: Skeld owed the demo-world narrative verdict; Boss owed an atlas redeploy after Captain commits.

## PRIOR STATE (2026-07-20 — SESSION 302: **THE PHASE KERNEL WIRED — phases live, the scripted CREATE DOOR, one create funnel, three shipped bugs closed**; gates: typecheck 0 + **1333/1333 vitest** + **full e2e 246/246, exit 0 direct, quiet machine, zero flaky** + build clean; UNCOMMITTED on top of the committed S301 pile)

Captain's rulings this round: share detour CUT, import CUT (a "more information" foldout at most), two eras with the pivot at 0 and no invented conversion, the calendar baked rather than taught, and the shorter step-0 draft. The contract's §7 amendments are now BUILT, not planned.

- **LANDING CAPTIONS RESTORED** (Captain's catch): S301 cut the two door TITLES (correct — they repeated their buttons) but took the description paragraphs with them, leaving two bare buttons. Each door now carries one muted caption ("one sentence becomes a small linked world" / "your worlds as plain files you keep"), no time claim, tool voice; the compact side-by-side look holds. Own-eyes: `Notes/ui-snapshots/2026-07-21/`.
- **THE STAGING MODEL REFACTORED (prep for the act restructure)**: chrome staging moved from the ordinal `data-tour-stage` number (derived from `step.act`) to CUMULATIVE capability classes declared per step (`unlocks: TourCapability[]` → `atlas-tour-cap-*` on body). The ordinal would have gone non-monotonic the moment the restructure interleaves acts (locations before objects → right pane flips on at a maps step, off at the next elements step). Capabilities only grow. `tourStageFor` deleted; ~14 CSS rules rewritten to `:not(.atlas-tour-cap-X)` / `.atlas-tour-cap-X`; the three e2e stage-number asserts (each redundant with a stronger visibility assert beside it) replaced with capability-class checks; three unit invariants lock it (caps only grow across the step order, every unlock is known, the tour reaches every capability). Behavior-preserving — full e2e green with acts unchanged, so the next bout opens straight onto content.
- **THREE SHIPPED BUGS, found by the critical read** (none had a test, all pre-date this session): (1) **the tour world's clock never reached state** — `scaffoldTourWorld` hand-built `active.meta` as `{id, name, demo}` while `writeWorldMeta` had just written four `time_*` fields, so `nowValue()` read null and the charts step promised "a gold line marking the now: year 15" over a room showing the "set now time" button. It self-healed on reload (activateWorld reads world.json), which is exactly why it survived: the bug only existed on the path every real user takes. Fixed by READING THE META BACK instead of re-listing fields, and e2e-locked as `mirror === disk` so any future meta field is covered without touching the test. Revert-checked. (2) **eleven of twelve create doors were unscripted** — DESIGN_TOUR claimed "during this beat EVERY create door is scripted" while `Ctrl+E` (`app.ts`) and the Charts room's + Element opened a free-form modal beside the locked one. (3) **`suppressToast` was a dead flag** — declared, passed, never read; it "worked" only because `overrideCreate` returns before the toast block.
- **PHASES ARE LIVE**: `TourStep.phases[]` replaces body/meta/deeper; the bar resolves via `tour-view.ts` (prefix semantics + suspension) and stamps `data-tour-phase`. The migration was proven copy-identical at RUNTIME (a temporary equivalence test against the committed table: every body, meta, deeper, route, reveal, handsOn, webTab and span verbatim) — a source-text fingerprint was tried first and was garbage, because a regex cannot tell a string literal from an apostrophe in a comment.
- **THE BAR SUBSCRIBES TO CORE STATE**, memoized on a signature that includes per-ref progress and lit-glyph state (R5 — without those terms, minting #2 of 3 changes nothing the signature sees and the middle click of a run produces NO feedback). Guards: renders suspend when the tour world is not active, and two engine flags (`enteringStep`, `tearingDown`) suppress mid-step and mid-teardown repaints (R4/R13). **The render memo resets on mount AND unmount and treats `root === null` as a miss** (R3, BLOCKER: exit-then-replay would otherwise mount a bar with no narrator, no Next, no Exit). `notifyTourPinPlaced` is GONE — the pin beat now re-renders off the ordinary state tick, verified by removing it and watching the pin specs stay green.
- **THE SCRIPTED CREATE DOOR**: `creates: string[]` on a step; the app's real + mints the next un-minted ref, one per click, no dialog. **All free-create entry points now funnel through `src/ui/create-element-door.ts`** (sidebar +, Ctrl+E, Charts + Element), so the tour branch exists in exactly one place and any miss falls through to the ORDINARY modal rather than leaving a dead button (R8). Mints serialize with the next ref computed inside the chain (R9). `TourSpan.ref` (R6) lights a glyph on its element's EXISTENCE, which collapsed the two hard-coded hands-on names in `buildSentence` into one predicate shared by the consul, the pin and every scripted create.
- **THE OBJECTS STEP IS THE FIRST RUN** (copy provisional, rides Captain's pass): four phases, three user mints, Next gated on all three. Own-eyes verified: one click = one element, one glyph, narrator advances, and phase d's copy is TRUE against the data (Possessions holds two of three because the balcony rides inside the ship). The gate's headline bug is closed and locked — `parent_object` survives, where the old strip rule left the step teaching that field on a page whose Parent object was empty.
- **A HIGHLIGHT NOBODY WOULD HAVE TESTED**: the run's copy says "the + above the element list", and the sidebar's own pulse only fires on an EMPTY world (S255v3) — so by the objects step it pointed at a control with no signal at all. Found by looking at the beat. `body.atlas-tour-createstep` gives it the same treatment the pin beat uses.
- **TWO REVERT-CHECKS THAT EARNED THEIR KEEP**: the double-mint spec passed with the serialization removed, because two awaited Playwright clicks are sequential enough that the first mint has already landed — it only reproduces the race when both clicks are dispatched SYNCHRONOUSLY in the page. And the R1 link assertion passed with half the fix reverted, because the write-time strip and the post-mint heal each repair it alone; both had to go before it failed. Neither is dead code, and the code now says so.
- **THE CLOSE GATE (3 opus lenses over the whole diff, ~401K) — 12 findings, all resolved**: 3 HIGH, and all three were THE SAME BUG found independently, which is the fix-first signal. **I built the entire suspension contract and then guarded away the only thing that can trigger it**: the bar's state subscriber bailed on `!tourWorldIsActive()`, which IS the world-switch transition suspension exists to paint. So `resolvePhase` returning phase A, `canAdvance` returning false and the signature's own `live|suspended` term were all unreachable — a mid-tour world switch froze the dock on tour narration over the user's own world, kept the tour's cyan pulse burning on THEIR sidebar +, and left a live Next that advanced the tour past a beat whose every write no-ops. Now regression-locked, revert-checked. Also fixed: a mint that reported success when `revealOne` had declined (the dead + button R8 exists to prevent); the async door window letting `Ctrl+E` stack modals and letting a fallthrough modal open after Exit tore the world down; `enteringStep` not covering the tour's OWN emitter, so the panel still repainted the new step before its reveal and route landed (my comment claimed otherwise — the closing emit moved out of `enterStepInner` so it fires after the flag clears); the signature covering the hands-on gate only BY COINCIDENCE (it worked because both beats duplicate their ref into a sentence span); a dead world-blind `tourPinPlaced` export; and `scaffoldTourWorld` now uses `writeWorldMeta`'s own return rather than a read-back that falls back to `{id:'',name:''}`. ~24 upheld-with-evidence.
- **THE GATE'S FIX COLLIDED WITH A FEATURE, AND THE SUITE CAUGHT IT**: the in-flight guard added for stacked modals also swallowed the second click of a scripted run — but a run is SUPPOSED to take rapid clicks, one element per click. The guard moved from the dispatch to the modal-open moment. The double-mint spec failed exactly as designed.
- **THE FULL SUITE CAUGHT WHAT THE SPEC FILE DID NOT**: four sibling tour specs walked past the objects step with `nextStep`, which no longer exists until three mints land — 45s timeouts, my own change, invisible to the per-file run I had been using. Also re-learned: do not run a spec file while a full suite is still going in the background, then read the result as a verdict.

## PRIOR STATE (2026-07-20 — SESSION 301: **THE TOUR REWORK — two bugs closed, landing + chrome pass, and the PHASE KERNEL (gated)**; gates: typecheck 0 + **1323/1323 vitest** + **full e2e 241**; committed by Captain as "tour rework part 1/2")

Captain's rework dump (the whole tour, restructured) digested into `Notes/2026-07-20-s301-tour-rework-plan.md`; the critical read that preceded it is `Notes/2026-07-20-s301-tour-critical-read.md`; the phase contract + its gate amendments are `Notes/2026-07-20-s301-phase-contract.md`. Own-eyes snapshots: `Notes/ui-snapshots/2026-07-20/s301-tour-audit/`.

- **TWO REAL BUGS CLOSED**: (1) **the residue leak** — teardown now prunes `atlas_right_tabs` + `atlas_map_camera_state` for the tour world (the persisted tabs are written DIRECTLY, because right-pane early-returns when no world is active, so the render-time persist never fires); a tour-finisher's first real world used to open with a ghost "Hyperion" tab reading "This map isn't in the current world". E2e-locked WITH a precondition assert, revert-checked. DESIGN_TOUR's residue clause now carries a table naming every store the tour writes. (2) **the demo-key link bug** — `isDemoKey` (`core/constants.ts`) + a single `readOnly = opts.readOnly || isDemoKey(key)` normalization at the top of `linkExistingApiWorldInRoot`, so the registry flag, world.json mirror, store arm and engine attach all fire from one decision; the PIN is now structurally stripped for view-only links, which closes the trap where a PREFILLED PIN sailed a demo key into a WRITABLE registration that 403s on first push. Keel backstops it (demo range = READ scope). 5 unit tests incl. "legacy 10-digit keys must NOT match".
- **LANDING REWORK** (Captain's round): titles + boxes cut, buttons compact/lowercase, sigil links to onlyworlds.com in a new tab. Both placements built and shot (`20-landing-above` / `21-landing-below`); shipped BELOW the tagline (mark → name → what it is → what you can do). Flip = drop two `order` rules.
- **TOUR CHROME PASS, measured**: right-pane occlusion **172px → 0** · dead space right of the sentence **~200px → 2px** · sentence size constant (the `.is-fresh` font-size override is gone; brightness only) · parsed-word underline removed and the type colour moved INTO the text (it lived only in the border, so deleting the border alone would have deleted the colour language) · unlit glyphs at 0.05 opacity · the "real element dialog…" meta cut · **create buttons removed from the widget entirely** (the app's own + is the only route; it already pulses in both cases) · tour-mode tooltips on the four greyed sidebar icons, which needed `aria-disabled` instead of `disabled` because a browser fires NO hover on a disabled or pointer-events:none control — the tooltips would have been written and never seen.
  - **The occlusion mechanism**: the shell reserved 180px while the dock occupied 306. Both now derive from `--tour-dock-h`, so they cannot drift again. **Trade to know**: the dock's height is now a direct tax on app space (446px of app at 1366×768), and there is ~92px of pure slack inside the dock — the copy pass is worth real estate, not just polish.
- **THE ZERO-SPAN AXIS FIX** (helps every young world, not just the tour): one dated element used to land at ~1.9% across, jammed against the left edge (`max = min + 100` then 2% padding). A zero-span range now CENTERS as `[d-50, d+50]`. The old unit test asserted only `max > min` — a wide assertion accommodating the bug; replaced with three that assert centering.
- **THE PHASE KERNEL (contract 3-lens gated BEFORE build — 21 findings, all folded as an amendments layer)**: `src/tour/tour-view.ts` — store-free, DOM-free `TourWorldView` + `resolvePhase` + `canAdvance` (13 unit tests). Decisions the gate reshaped: **prefix semantics** over last-match (deleting one element mid-step used to leave a later phase's copy claiming a deed), and **full suspension** when the tour's world is not active (a mid-tour world switch had the narration reading the user's OWN world, both collapsing to phase A and resolving TRUE against foreign data).
- **THE STRIP INVARIANT CHANGED (gate R1/R2, convergent ×2)**: the CREATE set stays step-keyed, but the LINK-REFERENCE set is now step-keyed ∪ what is on disk (`linkableIds`). The rule is **never strip a link whose target is present on disk** — stripping only ever existed to prevent dangling chips. Kills two bugs: user-minted elements losing links to each other (the objects step would have taught "the balcony's parent object is the spaceship" on a page with an empty Parent object), and back-navigation deleting chips off elements that still exist.
- **A TEST THAT PASSED BY ACCIDENT, CAUGHT BY ITS OWN REVERT-CHECK**: the back-nav regression synced on `data-tour-step`, which flips at `writeTourState`'s emit — BEFORE `revealUpTo` heals. It read the world mid-step and passed with the fix reverted. Fixed with a real settle signal: `awaitTourSettled()` (engine) exposed on the dev test API, mirroring `awaitTidies`. This also confirms gate finding R13 empirically in shipped code: the panel repaints the new step's copy before that step's reveal and route land.
- **TWO SHIPPED TESTS WERE LYING GREEN**: the OPFS spec asserted no button reading "Set up your folder" — a string the app has never rendered, so it would have stayed green if a picker button appeared on a browser with no picker. Fixed to the real label, and the full walk now asserts the SAME string is present under folder mode, so the pair is self-verifying. The finale's control set (Finish + folder, no Exit) had zero coverage; asserted positively now, before any migration touches that ladder.
- ⚡ **NEXT** (was: the creates door + phase wiring — DONE in S302): the act restructure (step 0, locations/map before objects so "sat" lights in reading order, institution+title combined), then writing and charts, then Captain's copy pass. **Before the restructure**: `tourStageFor` computes chrome staging from `step.act` arithmetic and atlas.css hardcodes `[data-tour-stage="4"]`-style include lists, so reordering acts silently re-keys both — replace the number with a declared cumulative capability set per step. Held for Captain: the copy pass, the charts-step look. CUT by his ruling: the share detour, the import detour.

- **THE change_seq SYNC BUG KILLED (Captain's live 422)**: keel's v2 tail added per-element `change_seq` to read responses; the adapter's SERVER_MANAGED_FIELDS strip missed it, so pulled records carried it to disk and every later PATCH 422'd "change_seq is read-only" — the element stuck in pending forever. ## PRIOR STATE (2026-07-19 — SESSION 295: **LAUNCH-DAY SPEED BOUT — body cache + parallel index + wire fixes, gated**; gates: typecheck 0 + 1270/1270 vitest + full e2e 223/223 zero-flaky exit 0 + 16/16 folder-store-fs incl. 3 new cache locks)

- **THE SPEED BOUT (Captain's #1: large worlds slow)**. Measured first (seeded 1,500-element OPFS world): cold open **18.5s**, every warm `listElements` **3.5s** (and `refreshElementsAfterWrite` runs one per edit). Audit fleet (4 dims + refute-verify, all findings CONFIRMED) + keeper trace agreed on the mechanism: the index pass parsed every body and threw it away, listElements re-read everything sequentially, and activation ran a third full walk for orphan-snapshot recovery that almost never fires.
- **Shipped**: `bodyCache` in FsFolderStore (index pass keeps bodies; serve = structuredClone; every write sink updates it — SYNC_NOTES "body cache" section holds the rules) · one-enumeration handle collection + 64-way parallel reads + cross-type concurrent builds (in-flight guard per type) · orphan-recovery returns boolean, re-prime only on true · progress: `listElements(opts.onProgress)` via store-level `indexTick` listener → "Opening world… 600 / 1,500" (count-throttled every 100; <100 elements never flashes) · adapter list walks send `limit=500` · initial download walks types 6-concurrent · meta step `skipIfLocalClean` on push rounds (autosaves skip the GET /world; SYNC_NOTES documents the deliberate freshness narrowing).
- **Measured after**: cold open **~3.2s** (pure store 2.2s ≈ the raw parallel floor), warm listElements **15ms**, progress counter verified streaming with own eyes (MutationObserver, 0→1,500 in steps of 100).

## PRIOR STATE (2026-07-13 — SESSION 261: **CHARTS ACT 2A SHIPPED — per-type charts as two mechanisms + card vocabulary** + the OnlyWorlds-folder copy sweep; gates: typecheck + **1081/1081 vitest, exit 0 direct** + **full e2e 193 passed, 0 flaky, exit 0 direct, quiet machine**)

**ACT 2A (S8 per-type charts) IS BUILT — REVISED BEFORE BUILD by schema evidence** (design record: `Forge/Notes/2026-07-13-act2a-design-revision.md`). The opener's four archetypes collapsed: character/creature have NO death field, family has NO dates, institution founding-only — so "lifespan birth→death" was schema-impossible. Live baselines showed the real gaps (ongoing title = a point; focused fit ignored the now; card meta = a bare number). Shipped as:

- **Recorded open-ended spans (all views)**: `title` carries `openEnd` in `TEMPORAL_TYPE_FIELDS` — grant with no revoke draws a bar to the now with a **fade-out cap** (the fade IS the honesty), lane-claimed to the effective end so points can't sit ON a held tenure. Engages ONLY when `time_current` is set and ahead of the grant. An INVERTED revoke (recorded bad data) stays a point — never an invented ongoing tenure. Kernel: pure `effectiveEndDate` (temporal-fields.ts).
- **Focus-inferred spans (the subject)**: `extras.focusId` → the focused node **places first in the beeswarm (holds the centre spine), its span claims the lane, and draws THICK**; a focused character/creature born before the now gets a **DOTTED birth→now life-line** (`focusInferredEnd`) — dotted = inference (schema records no death), focus-only (broad view would be a wall of bars converging on the now-line). **Narrative arcs + event beats fell out free**: a focused narrative = thick straddle-split ribbon with its involved elements beeswarmed along it. Fit extension: `extendRangeForOpenEnds` + focus-inferred — the now is always ON SCREEN when an open span runs to it (baseline finding: the ongoing-title focus fit stopped at its own grant).

**What shipped (S257)**:
- **`/bulk` push path** (engine `bulkCreatePhase` + Route 2 merge `processBulkCreates`): CREATE batches ≥2 = ONE atomic request, server fixpoint FK resolution incl. CYCLES — the cyclic-Make-Online class (Sikelia's 152-location web) is closed; 700-item single call probed. Atomic rollback verified live (201-status items after errors:true are NOT persisted → stamp nothing, retry survivors once). Timestamp backfill (≤20 → per-element GET; else list walk) until Skeld's per-item `updated_at` echo lands (accepted, next keel session — delete the backfill then). Topo-sort = fallback-only. **Two-phase push spec: superseded, never needed.**
- **`/changes` cursor pull** (`pullPhaseChanges`; legacy full-list = fallback): explicit delete ops replace absence-inference server-delete detection. 3-lens gated BEFORE wiring (7th consecutive earn: 13 findings, 5 HIGH — delete-then-recreate phantom-review data-loss, cursor-past-dropped-side-effects, unlink racing the walk, cross-endpoint timestamp skew, lost push triggers). All resolutions built + regression-locked (`sync-engine.changesPull.test.ts`); design + resolutions in `Notes/2026-07-12-v2-changes-pull-design.md`. ⚠ /changes paginates via **`since=`** not `cursor=` (unknown params silently ignored — the walker verifies cursor advance).
- **Structured error parsing**: typed-first (`invalid_link` routes dangling peers to review on CREATE **and UPDATE and /bulk items**; `id_conflict` self-heals by adopting the server baseline; `invalid_credentials`/`key_revoked` auth-bail; `rate_limited` is NOT auth), legacy-regex fallback. The staleness took three layers, each now fixed + regression-guarded on Anchor's side: (1) undeployed max-age split on PythonAnywhere → deployed; (2) browser-cache semantics → `max-age=0, s-maxage=N`; (3) **the Vary:origin purge gap** (my testyo evidence: purge-by-URL missed the with-Origin slot; edge-terminated 304s pinned viewers to it) → cf_purge.py purges BOTH variant slots (Anchor validated against the real CF API; my curl pair confirms both slots identical). NOTE: Anchor REFUSED dropping Vary:origin — correctly: django-cors-headers emits per-origin ACAO, so a shared cache slot would leak the allow-header to disallowed origins. The Vary is load-bearing; purge respects it.
